e:T47300,<h1 id="json-contracts">JSON contracts</h1>
<h2 id="one-versioned-system-schema">One versioned system schema</h2>
<p>insonic uses <a href="https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12">JSON Schema Draft 2020-12</a> for configuration, import, artifact/metadata interchange, durable job events, graph queries and speaker-training/model manifests. A system-wide master schema contains a registry of all insonic-owned contracts and selects documents through their <code>kind</code>. Shared definitions provide consistent identifiers, digests, revisions and artifact references. Cueson retains its own immutable upstream subtitle schema; insonic references its validated artifacts without redefining that format.</p>
<p>The master and child schemas live in <code>schemas/v&lt;version&gt;/</code>. Every release has the same software, documentation and master-schema version. A document declares <code>schema_version</code> and <code>kind</code>; the registry resolves its exact child contract. Tagged canonical schema IDs identify release resources, and the packaged registry resolves them offline. Schema files carry descriptions and examples for fields and complete documents. The documentation build reads those fields to produce the contract reference below and packages the JSON files beside it.</p>
<h2 id="compatibility-and-evolution">Compatibility and evolution</h2>
<p>Preserve backward and forward compatibility where practical. Prefer additive optional fields, stable meanings, explicit extension containers and migrations over silently changing existing values. Readers validate against the document's declared version and negotiate adapter capabilities. An unknown version is reported clearly; it is not silently interpreted with whichever schema is newest. Keep supported older registries available for imports and migrations. Unsupported features can remain preserved as opaque extension values where the contract permits them.</p>
<p>Compatibility is a priority, not an absolute prohibition on useful change. When a change breaks a consumer or persisted contract, describe the affected fields, supported source versions and migration/recovery path in the changelog and the brief release highlights. Release notes retain the final changelog link. A schema revision alone does not prove compatibility; validation and representative import/export/migration fixtures establish the supported combinations.</p>
<h2 id="validation-and-documentation">Validation and documentation</h2>
<p>The repository checks master registration, matching release identities, schema validity, field descriptions and embedded examples. Every document example must validate both against its child schema and the master. Contract checks also reject malformed identities, contradictory inputs and mismatched kinds/versions. Structural validation is complemented by application checks for source ownership, time bounds, permissions, revision ordering and backend capabilities; JSON Schema cannot prove those relationships by itself.</p>
<p>The generated reference is read-only output. Edit descriptions, examples and field constraints in the authoritative JSON files, then rebuild documentation. The same packaged definitions serve CLI validation, adapter interchange and offline help. <a href="/docs/v0.0.0/schema/">Catalog relationships</a>, <a href="/docs/v0.0.0/ingestion/">import behavior</a> and <a href="/docs/v0.0.0/voice-models/">speaker model lineage</a> explain the domain rules around these payloads.</p>
<h2 id="contract-reference">Contract reference</h2>
<p>The versioned root of all insonic-owned interchange contracts. Each document identifies its kind and system schema version. Software, documentation and this registry share the same release version. Stable required fields define each operation while documented extension containers support adapter-specific values. Cueson documents retain their separate immutable upstream schema rather than being rewritten as an insonic format.</p>
<p><a href="/schemas/v0.0.0/master.schema.json">Download the master schema</a> · <a href="/schemas/v0.0.0/common.schema.json">Download shared definitions</a></p>
<p>The following definitions, descriptions and examples are generated from the versioned JSON Schema files during the documentation build.</p>
<h2 id="insonic-immutable-artifact-manifest">insonic immutable artifact manifest</h2>
<p>Portable inventory of complete immutable bytes. Hosted model handles without downloadable bytes belong in the speaker-model contract and are not invented digest entries here.</p>
<p><a href="/schemas/v0.0.0/artifact-manifest.schema.json">Download artifact-manifest.schema.json</a></p>

<h3 id="artifacts">artifacts</h3>
<p>Complete artifact entries. Application validation additionally rejects repeated artifact IDs with conflicting content identity.</p>
<p>Value: array.</p>

<p>All of the following constraints apply:</p>
<p>When <code>storage_type</code> is &quot;s3&quot;:</p>
<p>Value: object.</p>
<p>Required fields: <code>bucket</code>.</p>
<p>Otherwise:</p>
<p>Disallowed combination: has <code>bucket</code>.</p>

<h2 id="insonic-saved-graph-query">insonic saved graph query</h2>
<p>Immutable saved query revision with either a portable QuerySpec or declared native dialect, typed parameters and explicit backend validation. Graph layout and pinned results are separate from evidence facts.</p>

<td></td>

<h3 id="example-1">Example</h3>

<h2 id="insonic-import-manifest">insonic import manifest</h2>
<p>Batch import input for the CLI and its GUI wrapper. Syntax validation precedes application validation of source access, calendar values, timezone/DST policy and admission. No approval per item is required for already configured routing.</p>
<p><a href="/schemas/v0.0.0/import-manifest.schema.json">Download import-manifest.schema.json</a></p>
<table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Field</th>
<th>Required</th>
<th>Value</th>
<th>Description</th>
<th>Examples</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>schema_version</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>Constant &quot;0.0.0&quot;</td>
<td>Version of the insonic document contract.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>kind</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>Constant &quot;import-manifest&quot;</td>
<td>Document discriminator used by the master registry.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>defaults</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>object</td>
<td>Batch defaults, overridden by explicit per-item fields. Date and timestamp are mutually exclusive within this object.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>items</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>array</td>
<td>Inputs in deterministic admission order, with independent partial-result reporting.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>extensions</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/extensions</td>
<td>Optional namespaced additions preserved without changing the core contract.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<h3 id="defaults">defaults</h3>
<p>Batch defaults, overridden by explicit per-item fields. Date and timestamp are mutually exclusive within this object.</p>
<table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Field</th>
<th>Required</th>
<th>Value</th>
<th>Description</th>
<th>Examples</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>originated_at</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/wallTimestamp</td>
<td>Entered recording timestamp, distinct from import, publication and file modification times.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>originated_on</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/calendarDate</td>
<td>Entered recording date when the recording time is unknown.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>timezone</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/timezone</td>
<td>Timezone for unzoned recording timestamps; local is resolved once for the batch.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>dst_fold</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>&quot;earlier&quot;, &quot;later&quot;</td>
<td>Explicit choice of offset for an ambiguous repeated daylight-saving wall time.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>dst_gap</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>Constant &quot;shift-forward&quot;</td>
<td>Explicit policy to resolve a nonexistent wall time by shifting forward; absent means no silent correction.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>copy</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>boolean</td>
<td>True admits a managed copy into the selected artifact store; false keeps an explicitly tracked local reference.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>preset</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>string</td>
<td>Configured processing/import preset name or ID; does not embed provider configuration.</td>
<td>&quot;local&quot;</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>extensions</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/extensions</td>
<td>Optional nonsecret input annotations.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<p>Disallowed combination: has <code>originated_at</code>, <code>originated_on</code>.</p>
<h3 id="items">items</h3>
<p>Inputs in deterministic admission order, with independent partial-result reporting.</p>
<p>Value: array.</p>
<h3 id="items-1">items[]</h3>
<p>One ordered source input. Per-item fields override batch defaults. Source and subtitle paths resolve relative to the manifest unless explicitly absolute.</p>
<table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Field</th>
<th>Required</th>
<th>Value</th>
<th>Description</th>
<th>Examples</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>source</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>string</td>
<td>Local source path or acquisition-adapter source locator. Credentials and temporary signed URLs must be resolved separately.</td>
<td>&quot;audio/session.wav&quot;</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>subtitle</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>string</td>

<td>&quot;video/session.srt&quot;</td>
</tr>
<tr>

<td></td>

<tr>

<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>timezone</code></td>
<td>No</td>

<td></td>

<td></td>

<h2 id="insonic-durable-job-event">insonic durable job event</h2>
<p>Versioned ordered event for a durable job/attempt. Event syntax does not establish a committed catalog or graph result: publication receipts/checkpoints and application state transitions retain that authority.</p>
<p><a href="/schemas/v0.0.0/job-event.schema.json">Download job-event.schema.json</a></p>
<table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Field</th>
<th>Required</th>
<th>Value</th>
<th>Description</th>
<th>Examples</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>schema_version</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>Constant &quot;0.0.0&quot;</td>
<td>Version of this insonic document contract.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>kind</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>Constant &quot;job-event&quot;</td>
<td>Document discriminator registered by the master schema.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>event_id</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/uuid</td>
<td>Idempotent event identity for replay/deduplication.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>workspace_id</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>

<td></td>
</tr>

<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>

<td>Yes</td>

<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>occurred_at</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>

<td></td>
</tr>

<td><code>event_type</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>&quot;queued&quot;, &quot;attempt-started&quot;, &quot;stage-started&quot;, &quot;progress&quot;, &quot;artifact-published&quot;, &quot;checkpoint&quot;, &quot;completed&quot;, &quot;failed&quot;, &quot;cancelled&quot;, &quot;publication-pending&quot;</td>

<td></td>
</tr>

<td><code>state</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>

<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>attempt_id</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/uuid</td>

<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>attempt_number</code></td>

<td></td>

<td></td>

<td><code>progress</code></td>
<td>No</td>

<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>artifact_refs</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>array</td>
<td>Immutable artifact references produced by this event, not temporary paths.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>

<td></td>

<td></td>

<h3 id="progress">progress</h3>
<p>Adapter-reported progress; absence is distinct from zero progress.</p>
<table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Field</th>
<th>Required</th>
<th>Value</th>
<th>Description</th>
<th>Examples</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>fraction</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>number</td>
<td>Fraction complete when the adapter supplies a meaningful denominator.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>completed_units</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>number</td>
<td>Nonnegative completed work quantity.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>total_units</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>number</td>
<td>Positive expected quantity when known; application additionally checks completed &lt;= total.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>unit</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>string</td>
<td>Unit for quantities, such as seconds, segments or training steps.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>message</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>string</td>
<td>Plain-language progress message excluding secrets and full source/transcript payloads.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

<p>All of the following constraints apply:</p>
<p>When <code>event_type</code> is &quot;queued&quot;:</p>
<table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Field</th>
<th>Required</th>
<th>Value</th>

<h2 id="insonic-early-media-metadata-snapshot">insonic early media metadata snapshot</h2>
<p>Immutable capture attempt bound to original media before transformations. It preserves raw extractor reports, qualified normalized observations, MIME interpretations and recording-date precision. Missing metadata differs from unsupported extraction or failure.</p>
<p><a href="/schemas/v0.0.0/metadata-snapshot.schema.json">Download metadata-snapshot.schema.json</a></p>
<table>
<thead>

<tr>

<td>Version of this capture contract.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>

<td></td>

<h2 id="insonic-pipeline-configuration">insonic pipeline configuration</h2>
<p>Immutable revision of a user-selected capability pipeline. CLI and GUI use this same configuration; local and hosted routes, preparation/training capabilities and credential IDs are explicit.</p>
<p><a href="/schemas/v0.0.0/pipeline-config.schema.json">Download pipeline-config.schema.json</a></p>
<table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Field</th>
<th>Required</th>
<th>Value</th>
<th>Description</th>
<th>Examples</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>schema_version</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>Constant &quot;0.0.0&quot;</td>
<td>Version of this public insonic contract.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>kind</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>Constant &quot;pipeline-config&quot;</td>
<td>Document discriminator selected by the master registry.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>pipeline_id</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/uuid</td>
<td>Stable pipeline identity across configuration revisions.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>revision</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/revision</td>
<td>Immutable pipeline revision selected by jobs.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>display_name</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>string</td>
<td>Human-readable pipeline name.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>stages</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>array</td>
<td>Stage declarations with stable IDs. Application validation constructs a dependency graph and verifies effective options.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>extensions</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/extensions</td>
<td>Optional namespaced nonsecret extension data.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<h3 id="stages">stages</h3>
<p>Stage declarations with stable IDs. Application validation constructs a dependency graph and verifies effective options.</p>
<p>Value: array.</p>
<h3 id="stages-1">stages[]</h3>
<p>One capability stage. Dependencies, adapter capability compatibility and binding existence are checked before a job starts.</p>
<table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Field</th>
<th>Required</th>
<th>Value</th>
<th>Description</th>
<th>Examples</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>stage_id</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>string</td>
<td>Unique stage ID used by dependencies and input bindings.</td>
<td>&quot;transcribe&quot;</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>capability</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>&quot;acquisition&quot;, &quot;metadata-capture&quot;, &quot;audio-prepare&quot;, &quot;transcription&quot;, &quot;diarization&quot;, &quot;subtitle-normalization&quot;, &quot;speaker-attribution&quot;, &quot;chunking&quot;, &quot;assertion-extraction&quot;, &quot;embedding&quot;, &quot;speaker-model-prepare&quot;, &quot;speaker-model-train&quot;, &quot;query-assistance&quot;</td>
<td>Processing capability requested from the chosen adapter.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>adapter_id</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>string</td>
<td>User-selected local or hosted adapter identity.</td>
<td>&quot;faster-whisper&quot;</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>adapter_contract_version</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>string</td>
<td>Exact adapter protocol version used for request/results.</td>
<td>&quot;1&quot;</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>execution</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>object</td>
<td>Explicit local or hosted routing; failure never selects an unconfigured host.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>credential_id</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/credentialId</td>
<td>Optional opaque credential ID available only to this stage.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>depends_on</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>array</td>
<td>Prerequisite stage IDs. Application validation rejects cycles and unknown IDs.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>inputs</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>object</td>
<td>Named adapter inputs resolved from admitted assets and earlier stage outputs.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>options</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/nonsecretOptions</td>
<td>Effective adapter options; adapter validation rejects unsupported values and secret fields.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>retry</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>object</td>
<td>Bounded idempotent retry policy, separate from changing provider routing.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>extensions</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/extensions</td>
<td>Optional stage metadata without hidden routing or secrets.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<h3 id="stagesexecution">stages[].execution</h3>
<p>Explicit local or hosted routing; failure never selects an unconfigured host.</p>
<table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Field</th>
<th>Required</th>
<th>Value</th>
<th>Description</th>
<th>Examples</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>mode</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>&quot;local&quot;, &quot;hosted&quot;</td>
<td>Where this stage executes.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>worker_id</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>string</td>
<td>Managed or user-selected local executable worker identity.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>provider_profile</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/backendProfileReference</td>
<td>Configured hosted provider profile, with credentials resolved separately.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<p>All of the following constraints apply:</p>
<p>When <code>mode</code> is &quot;local&quot;:</p>
<p>Value: object.</p>
<p>Required fields: <code>worker_id</code>.</p>
<p>Disallowed combination: has <code>provider_profile</code>.</p>
<p>Otherwise:</p>
<p>Value: object.</p>
<p>Required fields: <code>provider_profile</code>.</p>
<p>Disallowed combination: has <code>worker_id</code>.</p>
<h3 id="stagesdependson">stages[].depends_on</h3>
<p>Prerequisite stage IDs. Application validation rejects cycles and unknown IDs.</p>
<p>Value: array.</p>
<h3 id="stagesdependson-1">stages[].depends_on[]</h3>
<p>ID of a prerequisite stage in this pipeline.</p>
<p>Value: string.</p>
<h3 id="stagesinputs">stages[].inputs</h3>
<p>Named adapter inputs resolved from admitted assets and earlier stage outputs.</p>
<p>Value: object.</p>
<h3 id="stagesinputsname">stages[].inputs{name}</h3>
<p>Typed logical binding such as media.original or prepare.audio; never a shell expression.</p>
<p>Value: string.</p>
<h3 id="stagesretry">stages[].retry</h3>
<p>Bounded idempotent retry policy, separate from changing provider routing.</p>
<table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Field</th>
<th>Required</th>
<th>Value</th>
<th>Description</th>
<th>Examples</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>max_attempts</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>integer</td>
<td>Maximum attempts for eligible retryable errors.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>backoff_seconds</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>number</td>
<td>Initial nonnegative retry delay.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

<h2 id="speaker-training-dataset-snapshot">Speaker training dataset snapshot</h2>
<p>Portable immutable training corpus selection. Source, speaker, attribution, transcript and preparation revisions are frozen; later training preparation binds final bytes in a separate manifest.</p>
<p><a href="/schemas/v0.0.0/speaker-dataset.schema.json">Download speaker-dataset.schema.json</a></p>
<table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Field</th>
<th>Required</th>
<th>Value</th>
<th>Description</th>
<th>Examples</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>schema_version</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>Constant &quot;0.0.0&quot;</td>
<td>Version of this portable document contract.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>kind</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>Constant &quot;speaker-dataset&quot;</td>
<td>Discriminator identifying an immutable speaker training dataset snapshot.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>workspace_id</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/uuid</td>

<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>dataset_snapshot_id</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>

<td></td>

</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>selection</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>

<td>Complete frozen selection recipe and effective filters.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>preparation</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>Reference: #/$defs/preparation</td>
<td>Requested preparation contract frozen before final training inputs are materialized.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>members</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>array</td>
<td>Ordered included segment memberships; empty snapshots represent no matching corpus and do not imply runnable training.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>exclusions</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>array</td>
<td>Frozen excluded-segment decisions and their technical or user-supplied reasons.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>summary</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>Reference: #/$defs/summary</td>
<td>Frozen corpus totals and diagnostic summary.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>extensions</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/extensions</td>
<td>Namespaced dataset metadata preserved without altering immutable membership.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

<h3 id="definition-selection">Definition: selection</h3>

<h2 id="speaker-model-version-manifest">Speaker model version manifest</h2>
<p>Portable exact-version model descriptor preserving the originating speaker, frozen training corpus, preparation, artifacts/checkpoints, provider identity, declared compatibility and license metadata. Current family associations and availability remain revisioned catalog state.</p>
<p><a href="/schemas/v0.0.0/speaker-model.schema.json">Download speaker-model.schema.json</a></p>

<p>Value: Reference: #/$defs/licenseDeclaration.</p>

</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>extensions</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/extensions</td>
<td>Namespaced checkpoint compatibility facts.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<h3 id="definition-checkpointartifact">Definition: checkpoint.artifact</h3>
<p>Optional verified checkpoint bytes.</p>
<p>All of the following constraints apply:</p>
<h3 id="definition-checkpointartifact-alternative-1">Definition: checkpoint.artifact: Alternative 1</h3>
<p>Optional verified checkpoint bytes.</p>
<p>Value: Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/artifactReference.</p>
<h3 id="definition-checkpointartifact-alternative-2">Definition: checkpoint.artifact: Alternative 2</h3>
<p>Value: object.</p>
<p>Required fields: <code>sha256</code>, <code>byte_length</code>.</p>
<h3 id="definition-checkpointbasemodel">Definition: checkpoint.base_model</h3>
<p>Required base-model identity for resume, or null when none is required.</p>
<p>At least one of the following alternatives must match:</p>
<h3 id="definition-checkpointbasemodel-alternative-1">Definition: checkpoint.base_model: Alternative 1</h3>
<p>Exact base-model requirement for resume.</p>
<p>Value: Reference: #/$defs/baseModel.</p>
<h3 id="definition-checkpointbasemodel-alternative-2">Definition: checkpoint.base_model: Alternative 2</h3>
<p>Value: null.</p>
<p>At least one of the following alternatives must match:</p>
<h3 id="definition-checkpoint-alternative-1">Definition: checkpoint: Alternative 1</h3>
<p>Value: object.</p>
<p>Required fields: <code>artifact</code>.</p>
<h3 id="definition-checkpoint-alternative-2">Definition: checkpoint: Alternative 2</h3>
<p>Value: object.</p>
<p>Required fields: <code>hosted_handle</code>.</p>
<h3 id="definition-licensedeclaration">Definition: licenseDeclaration</h3>
<p>License/attribution statement preserved as metadata, with explicit unknown values rather than an invented eligibility policy.</p>
<table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Field</th>
<th>Required</th>
<th>Value</th>
<th>Description</th>
<th>Examples</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>scope</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>string</td>
<td>Material covered by the declaration, such as base-model, output or training-data.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>declared_license</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>At least one alternative</td>
<td>Declared license, or null when no declaration was supplied.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>attribution</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>At least one alternative</td>
<td>Supplied attribution text, or null when absent.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>source_basis</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>string</td>
<td>Origin of the declaration, such as upstream-manifest, provider-report or user-entry.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>source_artifact</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/artifactReference</td>
<td>Optional report/manifest artifact containing the declaration.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>extensions</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/extensions</td>
<td>Namespaced rights metadata as supplied by the upstream source.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<h3 id="definition-licensedeclarationdeclaredlicense">Definition: licenseDeclaration.declared_license</h3>
<p>Declared license, or null when no declaration was supplied.</p>
<p>At least one of the following alternatives must match:</p>
<h3 id="definition-licensedeclarationdeclaredlicense-alternative-1">Definition: licenseDeclaration.declared_license: Alternative 1</h3>
<p>Declared license expression or upstream name.</p>
<p>Value: string.</p>
<h3 id="definition-licensedeclarationdeclaredlicense-alternative-2">Definition: licenseDeclaration.declared_license: Alternative 2</h3>
<p>Value: null.</p>
<h3 id="definition-licensedeclarationattribution">Definition: licenseDeclaration.attribution</h3>
<p>Supplied attribution text, or null when absent.</p>
<p>At least one of the following alternatives must match:</p>
<h3 id="definition-licensedeclarationattribution-alternative-1">Definition: licenseDeclaration.attribution: Alternative 1</h3>
<p>Required or supplied attribution text.</p>
<p>Value: string.</p>
<h3 id="definition-licensedeclarationattribution-alternative-2">Definition: licenseDeclaration.attribution: Alternative 2</h3>
<p>Value: null.</p>
<h3 id="definition-evaluation">Definition: evaluation</h3>
<p>Method-qualified training/evaluation metrics; no unmeasured quality claim is implied.</p>
<table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Field</th>
<th>Required</th>
<th>Value</th>
<th>Description</th>
<th>Examples</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>method</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>string</td>
<td>Evaluation method identity.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>method_version</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>string</td>
<td>Version of the evaluation method.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>metrics</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>object</td>
<td>Named measurements reported by this method; absent measurements are not fabricated.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>report_artifact</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/artifactReference</td>
<td>Optional immutable evaluation report.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>extensions</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/extensions</td>
<td>Namespaced evaluation context without library content or credentials by default.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<h3 id="definition-evaluationmetrics">Definition: evaluation.metrics</h3>
<p>Named measurements reported by this method; absent measurements are not fabricated.</p>
<p>Value: object.</p>
<h3 id="definition-evaluationmetricsname">Definition: evaluation.metrics{name}</h3>
<p>At least one of the following alternatives must match:</p>
<h3 id="definition-evaluationmetricsname-alternative-1">Definition: evaluation.metrics{name}: Alternative 1</h3>
<p>Value: number.</p>
<h3 id="definition-evaluationmetricsname-alternative-2">Definition: evaluation.metrics{name}: Alternative 2</h3>
<p>Value: string.</p>
<h3 id="definition-evaluationmetricsname-alternative-3">Definition: evaluation.metrics{name}: Alternative 3</h3>
<p>Value: null.</p>

<h2 id="speaker-audio-segment-catalog">Speaker audio segment catalog</h2>
<p>Portable speaker segment query/export result. Source intervals and attribution revisions remain tied to immutable admitted media, across the whole workspace library.</p>
<p><a href="/schemas/v0.0.0/speaker-segments.schema.json">Download speaker-segments.schema.json</a></p>
<table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Field</th>
<th>Required</th>
<th>Value</th>
<th>Description</th>
<th>Examples</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>schema_version</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>Constant &quot;0.0.0&quot;</td>
<td>Version of this portable document contract.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>kind</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>Constant &quot;speaker-segments&quot;</td>
<td>Discriminator identifying this document contract.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>workspace_id</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/uuid</td>
<td>Workspace owning every segment and referenced catalog identity.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>catalog_revision</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/revision</td>
<td>Catalog revision observed by this result.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>generated_at</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>

<td>UTC instant when this result was generated.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>

<td><code>speaker_filter</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>At least one alternative</td>
<td>Requested speaker identity, or null for an unfiltered segment result.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>segments</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>

<h3 id="speakerfilter">speaker_filter</h3>
<p>Requested speaker identity, or null for an unfiltered segment result.</p>
<p>At least one of the following alternatives must match:</p>
<h3 id="speakerfilter-alternative-1">speaker_filter: Alternative 1</h3>
<p>Catalog speaker requested by this query.</p>
<p>Value: Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/uuid.</p>
<h3 id="speakerfilter-alternative-2">speaker_filter: Alternative 2</h3>
<p>Value: null.</p>
<h3 id="segments">segments</h3>
<p>Ordered source-linked segment records; an empty query result is valid.</p>
<p>Value: array.</p>
<h3 id="segments-1">segments[]</h3>
<p>Value: Reference: #/$defs/segment.</p>
<h3 id="nextcursor">next_cursor</h3>
<p>Pagination continuation token, or null when complete.</p>
<p>At least one of the following alternatives must match:</p>
<h3 id="nextcursor-alternative-1">next_cursor: Alternative 1</h3>
<p>Opaque continuation cursor without secrets.</p>
<p>Value: string.</p>
<h3 id="nextcursor-alternative-2">next_cursor: Alternative 2</h3>
<p>Value: null.</p>
<h3 id="definition-source">Definition: source</h3>
<p>Immutable original audio/video source and selected audio channels; no storage path or transient URL is carried here.</p>
<table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Field</th>
<th>Required</th>
<th>Value</th>
<th>Description</th>
<th>Examples</th>
</tr>

<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>media_entry_id</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/uuid</td>

<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>source_asset_id</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/uuid</td>

<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>source_artifact</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>All listed constraints</td>
<td>Byte identity of the admitted original media artifact.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>stream_id</code></td>

<td></td>
</tr>

<td>integer</td>

<td></td>

<td>Yes</td>
<td>array</td>

<td>Yes</td>
<td>string</td>
<td>Declared selection or mixing policy, such as preserve or mono-mix.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>interval</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/mediaInterval</td>
<td>Half-open source interval in original-clock microseconds; application validation enforces end_us greater than start_us and source-duration bounds.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>extensions</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/extensions</td>
<td>Namespaced adapter-specific source facts without paths or credentials.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>

</table>
<h3 id="definition-sourcesourceartifact">Definition: source.source_artifact</h3>
<p>Byte identity of the admitted original media artifact.</p>
<p>All of the following constraints apply:</p>
<h3 id="definition-sourcesourceartifact-alternative-1">Definition: source.source_artifact: Alternative 1</h3>
<p>Byte identity of the admitted original media artifact.</p>
<p>Value: Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/artifactReference.</p>
<h3 id="definition-sourcesourceartifact-alternative-2">Definition: source.source_artifact: Alternative 2</h3>
<p>Value: object.</p>
<p>Required fields: <code>sha256</code>, <code>byte_length</code>.</p>
<h3 id="definition-sourcechannels">Definition: source.channels</h3>
<p>Zero-based original channel indices used for this segment.</p>
<p>Value: array.</p>
<h3 id="definition-sourcechannels-1">Definition: source.channels[]</h3>
<p>Value: integer.</p>
<h3 id="definition-attribution">Definition: attribution</h3>
<p>Frozen attribution observation distinguishing a catalog speaker from a run-local acoustic voice.</p>
<table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Field</th>
<th>Required</th>
<th>Value</th>
<th>Description</th>
<th>Examples</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>attribution_id</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/uuid</td>
<td>Stable identity of this attribution observation stream.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>revision</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/revision</td>
<td>Exact immutable attribution revision used by this segment or dataset.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>speaker_id</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>At least one alternative</td>
<td>Assigned catalog speaker UUID, or null for an unresolved voice.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>speaker_identity_revision</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>At least one alternative</td>
<td>Speaker identity revision, or null when the speaker is unresolved.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>basis</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>string</td>
<td>Declared assignment basis, for example user, acoustic-comparison, reasoning or imported; custom adapter bases remain representable.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>method</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>string</td>
<td>Attribution method or adapter identity; does not imply independently measured accuracy.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>confidence</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>At least one alternative</td>
<td>Optional method-qualified confidence diagnostic, or null when absent.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>extensions</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/extensions</td>
<td>Namespaced attribution details; predictions and corrections remain distinguishable.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

</tr>

</table>

<h2 id="insonic-workspace-configuration">insonic workspace configuration</h2>
<p>Versioned nonsecret configuration selecting artifact, catalog and graph adapters independently. Filesystem/SQLite/LadybugDB are defaults, while S3/PostgreSQL/ArcadeDB have full planned application support.</p>
<p><a href="/schemas/v0.0.0/workspace-config.schema.json">Download workspace-config.schema.json</a></p>
<table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Field</th>

<h3 id="example-10">Example</h3>
<pre><code class="language-json"><span class="hljs-punctuation">{</span>
  <span class="hljs-attr">&quot;schema_version&quot;</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-string">&quot;0.0.0&quot;</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span>
  <span class="hljs-attr">&quot;kind&quot;</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-string">&quot;workspace-config&quot;</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span>
  <span class="hljs-attr">&quot;workspace_id&quot;</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-string">&quot;11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111&quot;</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span>
  <span class="hljs-attr">&quot;display_name&quot;</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-string">&quot;My library&quot;</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span>
  <span class="hljs-attr">&quot;control_directory&quot;</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-string">&quot;/home/user/MediaLibrary&quot;</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span>
  <span class="hljs-attr">&quot;default_import_timezone&quot;</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-string">&quot;local&quot;</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span>
  <span class="hljs-attr">&quot;profiles&quot;</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-punctuation">{</span>
    <span class="hljs-attr">&quot;storage&quot;</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-punctuation">{</span>
      <span class="hljs-attr">&quot;profile_id&quot;</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-string">&quot;33333333-3333-4333-8333-333333333333&quot;</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span>

<h2 id="shared-definitions">Shared definitions</h2>
<p>Shared logical types for the v0.0.0 contract baseline. This resource defines reusable values and is not a standalone application document envelope. Format annotations require a format-aware validator; semantic comparisons and timezone resolution remain application validation.</p>
<h3 id="definition-uuid">Definition: uuid</h3>
<p>Stable application-generated UUID identity. UUIDs identify logical records, not database row IDs or filesystem locations.</p>
<p>Value: string.</p>
<p>Example:</p>
<pre><code class="language-json"><span class="hljs-string">&quot;11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111&quot;</span>
</code></pre>
<h3 id="definition-sha256">Definition: sha256</h3>
<p>Lowercase SHA-256 digest of the complete immutable byte sequence. Provider ETags and multipart/composite checksums are not this digest.</p>
<p>Value: string.</p>
<p>Example:</p>
<pre><code class="language-json"><span class="hljs-string">&quot;0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef&quot;</span>
</code></pre>
<h3 id="definition-revision">Definition: revision</h3>
<p>Monotonically increasing logical revision within one entity or ordered revision stream. Expected-revision checks prevent lost updates; database-native sequences are not exposed as identity.</p>
<p>Value: integer.</p>
<p>Example:</p>
<pre><code class="language-json"><span class="hljs-number">1</span>
</code></pre>
<h3 id="cueson-compatible-timestamp">Cueson-compatible timestamp</h3>
<p>Absolute instant in Cueson's timestamp format: RFC 3339 text and Unix nanoseconds representing the same instant. The application verifies agreement and preserves integer precision; import wall-time literals and media-relative intervals use distinct types.</p>
<table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Field</th>
<th>Required</th>
<th>Value</th>
<th>Description</th>
<th>Examples</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>iso</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>string</td>
<td>RFC 3339 representation of the instant, including its UTC offset and available fractional-second precision. Audit output uses UTC.</td>
<td>&quot;2026-10-04T18:30:00.123456Z&quot;; &quot;1970-01-01T00:00:00.123456789Z&quot;</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>unix_ns</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>integer</td>
<td>Nanoseconds since the Unix epoch for the same instant as iso. Parse and serialize this integer without floating-point rounding; JSON Schema validation alone does not verify agreement with iso.</td>
<td>123456789</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<p>Example:</p>
<pre><code class="language-json"><span class="hljs-punctuation">{</span>
  <span class="hljs-attr">&quot;iso&quot;</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-string">&quot;1970-01-01T00:00:00.123456789Z&quot;</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span>
  <span class="hljs-attr">&quot;unix_ns&quot;</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-number">123456789</span>
<span class="hljs-punctuation">}</span>
</code></pre>
<h3 id="definition-calendardate">Definition: calendarDate</h3>
<p>Calendar date with day precision, formatted YYYY-MM-DD. A date does not assert a known recording time.</p>
<p>Value: string.</p>
<p>Example:</p>
<pre><code class="language-json"><span class="hljs-string">&quot;2026-10-04&quot;</span>
</code></pre>
<h3 id="definition-walltimestamp">Definition: wallTimestamp</h3>
<p>Entered timestamp literal with seconds and optional microseconds, Z or numeric offset. Without a zone, the captured import timezone policy applies. Calendar validity, DST folds/gaps and zone-offset agreement require application validation.</p>
<p>Value: string.</p>
<p>Example:</p>
<pre><code class="language-json"><span class="hljs-string">&quot;2026-10-04T14:30:00&quot;</span>
</code></pre>
<p>Example:</p>
<pre><code class="language-json"><span class="hljs-string">&quot;2026-10-04T18:30:00Z&quot;</span>
</code></pre>
<h3 id="definition-timezone">Definition: timezone</h3>
<p>Timezone selector: local, UTC, an IANA identifier or a numeric offset. This pattern validates shape, not IANA membership. Resolve local once and persist its actual interpretation.</p>
<p>Value: string.</p>
<p>Example:</p>
<pre><code class="language-json"><span class="hljs-string">&quot;local&quot;</span>
</code></pre>
<p>Example:</p>
<pre><code class="language-json"><span class="hljs-string">&quot;UTC&quot;</span>
</code></pre>
<p>Example:</p>
<pre><code class="language-json"><span class="hljs-string">&quot;America/New_York&quot;</span>
</code></pre>
<p>Example:</p>
<pre><code class="language-json"><span class="hljs-string">&quot;+02:00&quot;</span>
</code></pre>
<h3 id="definition-mediainterval">Definition: mediaInterval</h3>
<p>Positive-duration, half-open original-media interval in integer microseconds. The application must additionally validate end_us &gt; start_us and probed-duration/codec tolerance.</p>
<table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Field</th>
<th>Required</th>
<th>Value</th>
<th>Description</th>
<th>Examples</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>start_us</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>integer</td>
<td>Inclusive interval start on the original media clock in microseconds.</td>
<td>0</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>end_us</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>integer</td>
<td>Exclusive interval end on the same original clock in microseconds.</td>
<td>1250000</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<p>Example:</p>
<pre><code class="language-json"><span class="hljs-punctuation">{</span>
  <span class="hljs-attr">&quot;start_us&quot;</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-number">0</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span>
  <span class="hljs-attr">&quot;end_us&quot;</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-number">1250000</span>
<span class="hljs-punctuation">}</span>
</code></pre>
<h3 id="definition-artifactreference">Definition: artifactReference</h3>
<p>Portable reference to an immutable logical artifact. Digest and size can accompany the stable ID when available; location/materialization and access credentials belong to separate runtime records.</p>
<table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Field</th>
<th>Required</th>
<th>Value</th>
<th>Description</th>
<th>Examples</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>artifact_id</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>Reference: #/$defs/uuid</td>
<td>Stable catalog artifact ID, independent of object key or local cache path.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>sha256</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>Reference: #/$defs/sha256</td>
<td>Expected complete-content digest when the caller has verified bytes.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>byte_length</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>integer</td>
<td>Expected length of the complete content in bytes, including zero-length nonmedia artifacts.</td>
<td>4096</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>extensions</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>Reference: #/$defs/extensions</td>
<td>Optional namespaced reference annotations without changing identity.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<p>Example:</p>
<pre><code class="language-json"><span class="hljs-punctuation">{</span>
  <span class="hljs-attr">&quot;artifact_id&quot;</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-string">&quot;22222222-2222-4222-8222-222222222222&quot;</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span>
  <span class="hljs-attr">&quot;sha256&quot;</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-string">&quot;0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef&quot;</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span>
  <span class="hljs-attr">&quot;byte_length&quot;</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-number">4096</span>
<span class="hljs-punctuation">}</span>
</code></pre>
<h3 id="definition-backendprofilereference">Definition: backendProfileReference</h3>
<p>Reference to a selected versioned backend profile. Profile records hold configuration and opaque credential references; application documents do not embed credentials.</p>
<table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Field</th>
<th>Required</th>
<th>Value</th>
<th>Description</th>
<th>Examples</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>profile_id</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>Reference: #/$defs/uuid</td>
<td>Stable configured backend profile ID.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>profile_revision</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>Reference: #/$defs/revision</td>
<td>Selected immutable profile revision.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>adapter_id</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>string</td>
<td>Adapter identity used to interpret the referenced profile.</td>
<td>&quot;filesystem&quot;</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>contract_version</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>string</td>
<td>Version of the adapter contract selected for this profile.</td>
<td>&quot;1&quot;</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<p>Example:</p>
<pre><code class="language-json"><span class="hljs-punctuation">{</span>
  <span class="hljs-attr">&quot;profile_id&quot;</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-string">&quot;33333333-3333-4333-8333-333333333333&quot;</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span>
  <span class="hljs-attr">&quot;profile_revision&quot;</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-number">1</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span>
  <span class="hljs-attr">&quot;adapter_id&quot;</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-string">&quot;filesystem&quot;</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span>
  <span class="hljs-attr">&quot;contract_version&quot;</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-string">&quot;1&quot;</span>
<span class="hljs-punctuation">}</span>
</code></pre>
<h3 id="definition-credentialid">Definition: credentialId</h3>
<p>Opaque credential ID resolved by SecretStore. This field never contains a password, API key, session token or secret-bearing connection URL.</p>
<p>Value: string.</p>
<p>Example:</p>
<pre><code class="language-json"><span class="hljs-string">&quot;11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111&quot;</span>
</code></pre>
<h3 id="definition-endpoint">Definition: endpoint</h3>
<p>Configured HTTP(S) service endpoint without URL user information, query tokens or fragments. Local HTTP is permitted explicitly; service credentials are separate opaque references.</p>
<p>Value: string.</p>
<p>Example:</p>
<pre><code class="language-json"><span class="hljs-string">&quot;https://objects.example.com&quot;</span>
</code></pre>
<p>Example:</p>
<pre><code class="language-json"><span class="hljs-string">&quot;http://127.0.0.1:2480&quot;</span>
</code></pre>
<h3 id="definition-nonsecretoptions">Definition: nonsecretOptions</h3>
<p>Adapter-specific, versioned options validated further against that adapter's capability/configuration schema. These properties are intentionally extensible and must not contain credential values.</p>
<p>Value: object.</p>
<p>Example:</p>
<pre><code class="language-json"><span class="hljs-punctuation">{</span>
  <span class="hljs-attr">&quot;sample_rate&quot;</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-number">16000</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span>
  <span class="hljs-attr">&quot;device&quot;</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-string">&quot;cpu&quot;</span>
<span class="hljs-punctuation">}</span>
</code></pre>
<h3 id="definition-extensions">Definition: extensions</h3>
<p>Optional namespaced extension data. Core consumers may preserve unknown extensions without interpreting them. Extensions must not redefine required fields, hide credentials or mutate immutable provenance.</p>
<p>Value: object.</p>
<p>Example:</p>
<pre><code class="language-json"><span class="hljs-punctuation">{</span>
  <span class="hljs-attr">&quot;example.org/note&quot;</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-string">&quot;Optional annotation&quot;</span>
<span class="hljs-punctuation">}</span>
</code></pre>
